Abstract: Soft metamaterials provide a promising platform for robotics, biomedical devices, and flexible electronics. The localized mechanical responses by nonuniform excitation are ubiquitous in soft materials, yet their controlled transmission across assemblies remains largely overlooked in metamaterial design, which critically constrains nontrivial functionalities with end-to-end and long-range deformation transmission. Here, we introduce an impedance-guided design framework that enables programmable transmission of localized deformation in modular soft metamaterials, achieving behaviors unattainable by intuitive design. By establishing a nonlinear model considering position-dependent interactions and integrating the concept of mechanical impedance within metamaterials, we regulate assembly-level transmission solely through unit-cell topology optimization. The resulting framework enables effective synthesis of module families, allowing both homogeneous and heterogeneous assemblies to be custom-built with markedly enhanced transmission characteristics. Leveraging the highly combinatorial and extensible design space, we physically realize diverse on-demand displacement manipulation architectures, including obstacle-bypassing modular soft-metamaterial assemblies, defect-tolerant soft gripping, and embodied signal processing. Beyond deformation programming, the reconfigurability and reassemblability of these soft modules can embed electric logic signals, enabling energy-efficient and low-latency information processing through compliant-switch-controlled mechanical LED displays and wearable finger-motion-sensing controllers. Our method provides fundamental insights into localized deformation transmission in modular soft metamaterials and establishes a scalable route toward embodied-intelligence material systems, particularly for soft-metamaterial-centric actuation, sensing, and collective computing.

</details>


### [140] [Instrumentation and field tests to evaluate a rollover risk estimator for mobile machinery with mobile tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09188)
*Lama Al Bassit,Bastien Laurent,Philippe Samuel Heritier,Romain Duval,Valérie Sulis,Roland Lenain*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文针对搭载移动工具或机具的农用机械比固定配置机械更易侧翻的问题，改进了现有的实时侧翻风险预测算法（主要基于LTR指标），并将其应用于自行式喷雾机。通过静态与动态实地测试，验证了算法捕捉LTR变化的能力，并获取了构建精确数值模型和高质量仿真测试所需的数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搭载移动工具的农用机械内部动力学特性使其比固定配置机械更易发生侧翻，而现有侧翻风险预测算法主要针对静态配置车辆。因此需要将这些算法适配到移动工具机械上，并进行实地测试以验证其有效性，同时获取构建精确数值模型所需的数据。

Method: 首先将现有实时侧翻风险预测算法（基于LTR指标）适配于搭载移动工具的机械；然后在一台自行式喷雾机上开展实地测试，包括静态测试（确定车辆参数）和动态测试（实时测量车辆位移和轮地作用力）；最后利用采集数据通过仿真回放车辆位移，生成模拟侧翻事故以评估算法效能。

Result: 初始结果表明，算法能够捕捉LTR的变化，从而表达横向和纵向侧翻风险；通过实地测试采集的数据可用于仿真回放，生成模拟侧翻事故并评估算法在事故预防中的有效性。

Conclusion: 本研究表明，针对固定配置车辆开发的基于LTR的实时侧翻预测算法能够成功适配并应用于搭载移动工具的农用机械，实地测试验证了其有效性，所获数据也为后续高精度仿真和算法评估奠定了基础。

Abstract: Agricultural machines that carry mobile tools or implements have an internal dynamic that makes them more prone to rollover risk than machines with fixed configurations. Previous research has led to the development of algorithms capable of predicting rollover risk in real time by estimating relevant metrics, primarily the LTR (Load Transfer Ratio), with a focus on vehicles with static configurations. This paper presents the adaptation of these algorithms for machines with mobile tools and describes the field tests, conducted on a self-propelled sprayer, to evaluate their effectiveness in predicting rollover. The tests also aimed to acquire the necessary data to build an accurate numerical model of the vehicle and produce high-quality simulated tests. This paper describes the chosen instrumentation, the test procedure, and the experimental design of these tests. These field tests include static tests and dynamic tests. Static tests determine vehicle parameters, while dynamic tests measure variables related to the machine displacement and wheel/ground forces in real time. The initial results demonstrate the algorithms' ability to capture variations in LTR, which express lateral and longitudinal rollover risk. The data collected through these tests will enable the vehicle displacements to be replayed by simulation, producing simulated rollover accidents and evaluating the effectiveness of the developed algorithms in accident prevention.

</details>


### [141] [Topology-Preserving Mesh Adaptation for Sharp-Interface Multiphase PFEM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09446)
*Félix Ruyffelaere,Michel Henry,Jonathan Lambrechts,Jean-François Remacle*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于粒子有限元法(PFEM)的全拉格朗日框架，通过动态网格自适应策略和节点空盘技术，实现了对任意数量不可混溶相的多相流模拟，在保持低节点数的同时精确追踪界面并解耦界面物理与网格尺寸，成功验证了16相Rayleigh-Taylor不稳定性的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统界面追踪方法存在数值扩散问题，且网格分辨率会过早主导拓扑变化，限制了多相流模拟的精度和灵活性。

Method: 采用全拉格朗日PFEM框架，引入动态网格自适应策略，为离散化界面的每个线段分配节点空盘，确保边属于Delaunay三角剖分，从而保持尖锐几何界面而不依赖经典约束三角剖分。

Result: 在标准多相流基准测试中精确匹配参考结果，同时保持极低的总体节点数；成功实现了具有挑战性的16相Rayleigh-Taylor模拟，展示了方法的可扩展性和几何通用性。

Conclusion: 该方法解耦了界面物理与网格尺寸，允许独立于用户定义网格尺寸集成亚网格物理模型来适当控制拓扑变化，为复杂多相流模拟提供了高效、精确的计算框架。

Abstract: This paper presents a robust, fully Lagrangian framework based on the Particle Finite Element Method (PFEM) capable of simulating multiphase flows with an arbitrary number of immiscible phases. Interface-tracking methods can sometimes suffer from numerical diffusion or allow the underlying mesh resolution to prematurely dictate topological changes. To address these limitations, we introduce a dynamic mesh adaptation strategy that naturally preserves sharp geometric interfaces without relying on classical constrained triangulation. A node-empty disk is assigned to each segment of the discretized interface, ensuring that the edge is part of the Delaunay triangulation. Our approach decouples the interface physics from the grid size, allowing the integration of sub-grid physical models to properly govern topological changes independently of the user-defined mesh size. The capabilities and accuracy of the framework are validated against standard multiphase benchmarks, closely matching references while maintaining a remarkably low overall node count. We demonstrate the scalability and geometric versatility of the method, in particular with a challenging 16-phase Rayleigh-Taylor simulation.

</details>


### [142] [A Truckload of Satoshis: Detecting and Measuring One-Way Arbitrage in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09491)
*Eugenio Nerio Nemmi,Tobias Lauinger,Paz Grimberg,Massimo La Morgia,Damon McCoy,Alessandro Mei*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从中心化交易所（CEX）匿名现货交易数据中推断单向套利（OWA）交易的方法，在币安（5年）和Kraken（9年）数据中分别识别出4.02亿和近200万条OWA序列，占总交易量的0.94%和0.13%，总利润分别为3120万美元和97.5万美元，但单序列平均利润不足1美元，且OWA速度加快而利润下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中心化交易所（CEX）支持数百种币种之间的快速链下兑换，但其公开交易数据不包含地址或交易者标识符，导致难以分析平台上存在的算法交易模式。现有研究缺乏有效方法从匿名化交易数据中识别套利行为。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的方法论，从CEX的匿名现货交易数据中推断单向套利（One-Way Arbitrage, OWA）交易序列，通过分析交易时间序列和价格差异来识别潜在的套利模式。

Result: 在币安5年数据中识别出4.02亿条OWA序列，占交易量的0.94%，总利润3120万美元；在Kraken9年数据中识别出近200万条序列，占交易量的0.13%，总利润97.5万美元。单序列平均利润不足1美元，且随时间推移OWA速度加快但单序列利润下降。

Conclusion: 中心化交易所中经常出现定价差异，为单向套利提供了机会，但个体套利利润微薄且呈下降趋势。研究结果对未来识别有利可图的OWA交易的具体条件提出了新的问题。

Abstract: Centralized cryptocurrency exchanges (CEXes) enable fast off-chain conversions between hundreds of coins. It is an open question which algorithmic trading patterns occur on these platforms. A major challenge to measuring CEXes is that their public trade data does not contain addresses or trader identifiers allowing linkage. We propose a novel methodology to infer one-way arbitrage (OWA) trading in anonymized spot trade data from CEXes. We identify 402 M likely OWA sequences in 5 years of trading on Binance (and almost 2 M during 9 years on Kraken), accounting for 0.94 % and 0.13 % of the total traded volume, respectively. While we estimate total profits of $31.2 M on Binance and $975 k on Kraken, profits from individual OWA sequences are less than $1 on average after accounting for trading fees. We also observe that OWA has become faster over time, while the profitability of individual sequences has decreased. Our findings highlight that pricing discrepancies regularly occur in CEXes, and raise questions for future work to identify the precise circumstances that enable profitable OWA.

</details>


### [143] [Artificial Intelligence and the Generative Science of Food Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09529)
*Vahidullah Tac,Ellen Kuhl*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的生成式食品配方科学框架，通过数字食品表征使人工智能能够预测、发现、生成、组织、模拟和优化食品配方，将可持续性和营养指标从事后评估标准转变为明确的设计目标，并推动计算食品设计成为一门严谨的科学学科。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统食品配方依赖经验实验、专家直觉和迭代改进，而现有AI在食品科学中的应用多为孤立的预测和优化任务。本文旨在建立一个统一的生成科学框架，整合AI能力，系统化地改变食品配方的设计范式。

Method: 提出了一个统一框架，核心是数字食品表征（digital food representations），使AI具备预测、发现、生成、组织、模拟和优化六种能力。通过可持续性和营养两个应用场景，展示生成式AI如何将环境和营养指标从事后评估转化为显式设计目标。

Result: 成功定义了生成式食品配方科学的框架结构，并以可持续性和营养为例验证了该框架的有效性，表明生成式AI能够将多目标约束（口味、营养、可持续性、成本）统一纳入设计过程。

Conclusion: 数据、模型、基准测试和自动化将推动计算食品设计成为一门严谨的科学学科，最终将食品配方从经验科学转变为生成科学。

Abstract: Food formulation requires balancing taste, nutrition, sustainability, and cost. Traditionally, new foods have emerged through empirical experimentation, expert intuition, and iterative refinement. Artificial intelligence is advancing rapidly across food science, yet most applications remain isolated prediction and optimization tasks rather than parts of a broader scientific framework. Here we define a unified framework for the generative science of food formulation, in which digital food representations enable artificial intelligence to predict, discover, generate, organize, simulate, and optimize. We illustrate this framework through sustainability and nutrition, where generative artificial intelligence transforms environmental and nutritional metrics from post hoc evaluation criteria into explicit design objectives. Finally, we identify the data, models, benchmarks, and automation that will establish computational food design as a rigorous scientific discipline. Together, these advances are transforming food formulation into a generative science.

</details>


### [144] [A novel robust mixed integer linear programming model for index tracking problem under no rebalancing: heuristic optimization approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09556)
*Danial Ramezani,Mostafa Abouei Ardakan,Mohamadreza Dehghani Ahmadabad*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种更鲁棒的指数追踪新模型，并开发了结合遗传算法与局部搜索的元启发式算法来高效求解NP-hard问题，实证表明该方法在样本内外数据上均优于商业求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动管理因低费率等优势日益流行，指数追踪旨在用少量资产复制指数表现。现有模型存在鲁棒性不足、样本外表现不佳等问题，且问题本身是NP-hard的，难以在多项式时间内求解。

Method: 提出一种新的指数追踪数学模型；设计了一种融合遗传算法的全局探索能力与局部搜索特性的元启发式算法（结合局部分支策略）。

Result: 在OR库数据上，该启发式算法在中小规模问题上能收敛到最优解，且生成的组合在样本内外数据上均优于商业求解器。

Conclusion: 所提模型更鲁棒，能长期追踪指数而无需频繁再平衡；所提算法能有效求解该NP-hard问题，综合性能优于商业求解器。

Abstract: Passive management has increasingly won popularity over the past few years because of its advantages, such as lower management fees and transaction costs. Index tracking endeavors to reproduce the performance of an index with smaller sets of assets. In this paper, a novel formulation is proposed that is not only more robust than the existing ones but also performs better on out-of-sample data and tracks indices over long periods without any considerable deviation or the need for rebalancing. Solving index tracking problems in a polynomial time is a challenging task due to their NP-hard nature. To address this issue, a novel heuristic based on metaheuristic algorithms and local branching is also developed to solve the proposed model. The heuristic enjoys not only the exploration capabilities of a genetic algorithm but the characteristics of local search algorithms as well. The data from the OR library is used to verify the capabilities of the proposed heuristic in comparison with commercial solvers. Results indicate that not only is the heuristic able to converge to optimal solutions for not-so-large problem sizes, but the portfolios it generates also outperform those yielded by commercial solvers in terms of both in-sample and out-of-sample data.

</details>


### [145] [Large-Scale Portfolio Optimization Problem Under Cardinality Constraint With Enhanced Multi-Objective Evolutionary Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09566)
*Danial Ramezani,Mostafa Abouei Ardakan*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文针对带基数约束的投资组合优化这一NP-hard问题，提出了一种改进的多目标进化算法策略，通过新型解表示、算子和修复机制实现了更快的收敛速度和更强的搜索能力，实验表明该方法在基准市场指数上优于传统算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融市场中可选资产数量日益增长，使投资者决策愈发困难。带现实约束（如基数约束）的投资组合优化是NP-hard问题，精确方法效率低下，因此需要更高效的进化算法来逼近最优解。

Method: 提出了包含独特解表示、新型算子和新修复机制在内的增强策略，并配合新的交配策略，集成到多目标进化算法中，以解决资产数量上下限约束下的投资组合优化问题。

Result: 在多个知名市场指数基准上的测试表明，所提策略不仅提供了更好的近似解，而且收敛更快，且随着市场资产数量增加性能不降。

Conclusion: 所提出的增强策略在多目标进化算法中有效提升了投资组合优化问题的求解质量和效率，具有良好的扩展性。

Abstract: Decision-making is posing an increasingly formidable challenge to investors because of the growing number of alternatives available in financial markets. A hot area of research over the past few decades has been portfolio optimization that seeks to determine how much an investor should invest in which asset. Introducing real-world conditions to the optimization model turns the problem into an NP-hard one for whose solution exact methods become inefficient; hence, researchers have turned to evolutionary algorithms to approximate solutions. In this paper, strengthening strategies are presented for multi-objective evolutionary algorithms that can provide a faster convergence rate and extensive search ability in the portfolio optimization problem under the cardinality constraint. To implement those features, a unique solution representation, a novel operator, and new repair mechanisms are introduced for solving the aforementioned problem in which lower and upper limits are set on the number of assets in the portfolio. For this purpose, new mating strategies along with the aforesaid package are implemented in well-known multi-objective evolutionary algorithms to solve the problem. The customized algorithms are subsequently tested against traditional ones using well-known market indices as benchmarks. Results indicate that the proposed strategy not only provides better approximations but also converges faster as well at no loss of performance with an increasing number of assets in the market.

</details>
